    Following live without hammering (RN-639)

    RN-639 — What changes per token does not re-render the screen, and what follows the bootstrap polls at 3 s and stops at the end

    Three screens did work nobody saw (AT-301/AT-302). The Session screen re-rendered ENTIRELY on every token of a turn — the in-progress text lived in the state of the hook it called. The provisioning and adoption screens polled the bootstrap status and the technical session's events at 1 s, and the events never stopped, even with the repository ready. And /status hit forever a service that did not respond.

    1. The in-progress text lives in an external store. The consultative chat text and the turn's activity strip stay in a per-session store (criarStoreDoStreaming), subscribed via useSyncExternalStore only by the two components that draw them — the thread bubble and the strip. The page subscribes to ONE boolean question (has the turn shown content yet?), which is what the "thinking" timer (RN-131) needs and which changes once per turn. The strip's reducer is the same; what changed is who holds the state.
    2. The bootstrap is followed at 3 s. Status and events, on both screens.
    3. And it stops at the end. Status and events stop when the bootstrap converges (provisioned) and when the query errors (pollQueParaNoErro). FAILURE (provision_failed) is terminal in adoption and NOT in provisioning: there "Tentar novamente" triggers a new bootstrap and the screen needs to see its progress (RN-477).
    4. /status stops on error. A service that responds — including with 5xx, which becomes status: 'error' in a resolved body — continues every 5 s. A service that does not respond stops being asked by timer; window focus and remount ask again, and the first success resumes the pace.
    • Where: apps/web/src/lib/streaming-do-turno.ts:48 (criarStoreDoStreaming), apps/web/src/lib/streaming-do-turno.ts:98 (useSemConteudoNoTurno), apps/web/src/lib/poll-do-bootstrap.ts:33 (pollDoBootstrap), apps/web/src/lib/poll-do-bootstrap.ts:47 (bootstrapTerminou), apps/web/src/routes/ProvisioningPage.tsx:41 (acompanharBootstrap), apps/web/src/routes/StatusPage.tsx:78 (pollQueParaNoErro)
    • Test: apps/web/src/routes/SessionPage.streaming-isolado.test.tsx (twenty tokens after the first do not re-render the page; the end of the turn re-renders), apps/web/src/lib/streaming-do-turno.test.ts, apps/web/src/lib/poll-do-bootstrap.test.ts, apps/web/src/routes/StatusPage.test.tsx
    • Origin: AT-301, AT-302

    Deciding in the chat, where the owner is (RN-626)

    RN-640 — The neutral palette closes the contrast debt, and UI color only comes from a token

    The dark theme stops being petrol blue and becomes neutral black with a soft terracotta accent; the light one becomes the neutral of the same family (ADR 0181). The mechanism of RN-184 does not change — the pairs are measured in both themes, with the same floor —; what changes is what is demanded:

    1. The dark theme's debt became a floor. The five pairs that RN-184 left locked by number (3.89 / 3.10 / 3.88 / 3.88 / 4.41) pass 4.5:1 in both themes, and the test rejects whoever brings them back down — it no longer "records". The final value came from MEASUREMENT: the draft's --text-muted (#71717a) repeated the debt and was refused (#86868f in dark, #696972 in light), and muted stays BELOW --text-secondary on the three surfaces, which the test also demands.
    2. Text over a solid brand/state background passes AA. The dark theme's --on-accent is the neutral black (5.61:1 over the accent, 7.25:1 on hover, 7.65:1 over --success, 5.24:1 over --danger); the "known exception" of the primary button ceases to exist, and the accent's hue stays terracotta (10°–30°) in both themes.
    3. UI color only comes from a token. Whatever paints through a runtime library (Mermaid, xterm, minimap) reads the active theme's token and, without it, falls back to the default of TOKENS_PADRAO — a copy of :root that the test checks against design/tokens.css. Agent color is always var(--token); the three agents without a semantic one have --agent-* per theme, measured at 3:1. And no CSS module uses a var(--x) that nobody declares.

    • Test: apps/web/src/lib/contraste.test.ts ("a antiga dívida de contraste agora é piso"), apps/web/src/design-contraste.test.ts, apps/web/test/design-contraste.test.ts ("o botão primário — a exceção que deixou de existir"), apps/web/src/lib/tokens-padrao.test.ts, apps/web/src/lib/agents.test.ts ("cor de agente vem do design system"), apps/web/src/design-tokens-existentes.test.ts

    Following live without hammering (RN-639)

    RN-643 — Below 768px the sidebar becomes a drawer and the project rail becomes a horizontal bar; the page never scrolls sideways

    The visual audit of Round 29 (AT-290, finding S1) measured that the product had no mobile layout after login: at 390px the sidebar (264px) and the project rail (180px) remained FIXED, adding up to 444px of frame, and the content was left with 0 to 126px or overflowed to the right. Configurações reached 114 truncated texts (validacao-visual.js).

    The rule:

    1. One cut, one source. The breakpoint is CONSULTA_MOVEL ((max-width: 767px)), read by useLayoutMovel. Whoever switches layout reads that hook and applies the mobile design through a CLASS; the CSS modules have no @media of their own for it. That way what the JS decides and what the CSS draws do not diverge at a boundary pixel, and the component test (jsdom, without CSS) proves the same cut the browser applies. Without matchMedia the layout is the DESKTOP one: mobile only applies when the query asserts it.
    2. The sidebar becomes a drawer. A bar at the top carries the menu button (aria-expanded, aria-controls) and the brand. The drawer is a modal dialog (role="dialog", aria-modal): on opening, focus enters it; Tab does not leave; Esc, the X and the backdrop close it and return focus to the menu button. It also closes on following any link inside it (the project tabs only change ?tab=) and when the route changes from outside. In the drawer the sidebar always appears EXPANDED, and the "Recolher menu" button disappears: manual collapse (RN-195) is a desktop preference, it stays recorded and applies again when the window crosses the cut back.
    3. The project rail becomes a horizontal bar. They are the same 12 leaves, in the same order, with the group headers inline and aria-orientation="horizontal". The arrows that move are left and right, and the active tab is scrolled into the visible strip. The bar scrolls INSIDE; the page does not scroll sideways. The project body becomes a column (the bar above the panel), and the Visão geral stacks the team and the activity instead of keeping the 360px rail beside them.

    Measured (390×844, dark theme, api seed, validacao-visual.js injected): the document's scrollWidth = 390 on all authenticated screens (dashboard, the 12 tabs, /containers, /account), and <main> with the whole 390px. In Configurações, texto-cortado dropped from 114 to 33. The rest is component text (tables and section labels), a matter of typography and space, not of frame. The fora-da-viewport left over in Visão geral and Aprovações are tabs of the horizontal bar beyond the edge, where internal scrolling reaches them.

    Revised by ADR 0211 (AT-404): item 3 no longer applies — the project rail left, and with it the horizontal bar. On the phone the project tabs live in the DRAWER (item 2), with the groups, the active one and the counters; choosing one closes the drawer. The project body no longer becomes a column: only the paddings shrink.

    What this rule does NOT close: the Código tab keeps the 48px rail of CodeShell and the editor in columns; it was not designed for the phone. The Session screen inherits the new frame, but the top bar and the thread still have findings X1–X3 of the audit. The duplication of tab navigation (drawer and bar) closed in ADR 0211.

    • Origin: AT-316 (finding S1 of AT-290); does not change ADR 0126, only gives the rail a form for narrow screens

    Deciding in the chat, where the owner is (RN-626)

    RN-644 — The thread cuts by MESSAGE, never splits an exchange and collapses the history in the order it happened

    The visual survey of Round 29 (finding X3, AT-319) measured three defects in the thread cut of RN-177, all in the same function: (1) the "last 5" counted entries, and the handoff, approval and story cards counted as one message each — in a short conversation, the first question and the first answer went to the collapsed history; (2) the cut fell where the count said, possibly leaving the question collapsed and the answer open; (3) the history was grouped by origin, in the order of the origins, and the "LLM" group came before "Usuário" — expanded, the Criativo's answer appeared ABOVE the question that generated it.

    The rule, in the thread (the log panel does NOT change — there the axis is the layer):

    1. The cut counts MESSAGES: chat.message, agent.response and chat.structured_question (TimelineEntry.mensagem). Cards and dividers do not count; those that come after the cut stay open with the messages around them. A per-agent collapse (RN-138) and a "Passos do turno" are still ONE entry, and count as one message.
    2. The cut never splits a turn (RN-172): it backs up to the opening of the turn of the message that marked it, so the question always stays together with the answer. That is why the open ones may exceed five — never an exchange in half. The prologue (turn 0) has no opening and does not back up; if backing up reaches the start, there is no history.
    3. The history is ONE block, in chronological order, and DECLARES the cut (RN-180): the title says what the cut counts ("Antes das últimas 5 mensagens") and the trailing separates what stayed inside ("2 mensagens · 2 outras entradas"). The "LLM" label leaves the thread along with the per-origin groups.

    What was lost, declared: reopening only what the user said, without reopening the rest, was the reason for the per-origin groups in the thread. It does not survive chronological order — the log panel keeps offering that cut.

    • Code: apps/web/src/routes/session-fio.tsx:119 (FIO_RECENTES_ABERTAS), :291 (dividirFio), :141 (entradaSimples); apps/web/src/lib/session-timeline.ts:67 (mensagem); apps/web/src/routes/session-timeline-montagem.tsx:389, :426, :673 (the three messages); apps/web/src/routes/SessionFio.tsx:139 (the single block)
    • Test: apps/web/src/routes/session-fio.test.ts (cards do not count, the cut backs up to the opening, thread order, and the failure case of a turn larger than the cut); apps/web/src/routes/SessionPage.painel-e-agrupamento.test.tsx (describe "RN-177/RN-644": the measured case with interleaved cards, and the expanded history in order)
    • Origin: AT-319, finding X3 of the "before" visual survey of Round 29

    Following live without hammering (RN-639)

    RN-645 — Configuration is not fetched again on every mount, does not poll, and the Configurações tab has three frame strips, not four

    The visual survey of Round 29 (finding G1, AT-321) went through the Configurações sections in ~40s and the project's whole page turned into "Limite de requisições excedido" — the 300 req/min ceiling is the USER's (RN-579). The script RELOADED the page per section, and one load of the tab cost 55 requests. Measured with the real screens, the defect had three parts, and none of them calls for raising the ceiling:

    The rule:

    1. CONFIGURATION data is valid for one minute (FRESCOR_DA_CONFIGURACAO_MS). With TanStack's staleTime: 0, every observer that MOUNTS after the first one refetches. The sections only mount after the project arrives, so GET /projects/:id, GET /projects/:id/git/repository and GET /workspaces went out TWICE on every load; and switching tabs and coming back remounted the 19 sections and redid 31 fetches. Configuration only changes by MUTATION, and every mutation of the tab invalidates its own key — invalidating ignores staleTime, so whoever saves sees the new value immediately. What the minute delays is a change made by ANOTHER person, which still arrives on window focus and on the next mount after the minute. Operational state stays out (container lifecycle, spend per agent and per key, device keys with lastUsedAt, bootstrap plan): there the question is "what is up now".
    2. Configuration does not poll. The proficiency profile and the instruction version history polled at 15s — 8 req/min against the ceiling for two lists that only change through their own section (which invalidates) or through an approved patch.
    3. The summary LIES DOWN above the sections, at any width. As a sticky column next to them it was the fourth vertical strip (sidebar 264 + rail 180 + summary 208): at 1440px ~740px of content remained and the models table cut the agent name at 79px and the model's at 91px (finding G2). The column had no way to pay its own price even on a wide monitor, because .body has a ceiling of 1040px (ADR 0078). Laid down, it gives the 208px back to the sections; the price is what the narrow window already paid — one loses the "where am I" STUCK to the screen, not the navigation. No token changed.

    Numbers (configuracoes.orcamento.test.tsx, Shell + ProjectPage on the Configurações tab over a fetch that counts per route; dev → this rule):

    Scenario Before After
    tab load (2.5s) 53 50
    one minute idle on the tab 76 68
    Configurações → Visão geral → back (configuration fetches) 31 0
    tab load, with the resolved bindings in a BATCH (RN-654, AT-334) 49 30

    The "After" of the first three rows were measured on the same base as the "Before"; integrated into dev with RN-638 (the frame reads the project queue only once), the load stood at 49 and the minute at 64. The fourth row is RN-654, measured on dev at 94e5dc721d: the 20 bindings resolved per key became ONE read, and the test holds the load at 30 and the minute at 64.

    What this rule does NOT close: until RN-654, 20 of the load's requests were the RESOLVED binding of each agent (17) and of each area (3), one route per key (GET projects/:projectId/agent-bindings/:agentSlug) — that part closed with the BATCH route. RELOADING the page per section, as the survey script did, still costs 30 per load (~10 loads per minute before the ceiling); navigating through the summary INSIDE the tab remounts nothing and costs zero. The 68 of the minute all come from the FRAME (the Shell polls projects-summary and execution/session at 5s and the execution session's events at 3s), common to every tab — the same gap that RN-632 declares.

    • Code: apps/web/src/lib/query-policy.ts:111 (FRESCOR_DA_CONFIGURACAO_MS); apps/web/src/lib/hooks.ts:29 (useCurrentWorkspaceWithRole), :535 (useProficiency); apps/web/src/routes/settings/InstructionVersionsSection.tsx:31 (instruction-versions, without poll); the sections of apps/web/src/routes/settings/ and apps/web/src/components/ModelCatalogSection.tsx; apps/web/src/routes/settings/sumario.module.css:36 (.layout, the strip)
    • Test: apps/web/src/routes/configuracoes.orcamento.test.tsx (the load fetches each resource once; one minute without a configuration poll; switching tabs and coming back without configuration fetches — all three fail on dev — and the failure case: invalidating fetches immediately, and once the minute has passed coming back fetches again)
    • Origin: AT-321 (findings G1 and G2 of the visual survey of Round 29; extension of RN-579 and RN-632)

    Deciding in the chat, where the owner is (RN-626)

    RN-646 — A block and an inert control state the reason of WHOEVER is on screen: the PRs tab speaks of the PR list, and Parar/Remover without a container say there is no container

    Two screens showed a correct state with the text of another place (AT-323, AT-324 — findings P1 and K1/K2 of the visual survey of Round 29).

    The PRs tab and the image gate. The block is REAL and stays: the PR list and the diff go through the SAME funnel as the Código tab (ReadProjectCodeUseCase.alvo → portaoDoContainer), so without a decided image (RN-105, in all three modes by RN-494) the api responds 409 there too — measured, not assumed. What was wrong was the text: the PRs tab showed "A aba Code ainda não está liberada", speaking of ANOTHER tab and with a name ("Code") that is not the rail's ("Código"). Now ContainerImageGateNotice receives the blocked SURFACE ('code' | 'prs'), the PRs tab says "A lista de PRs ainda não está liberada" and explains that it reads the repository through the same path as the Código tab; and the tab names in the text come from nav:tabs.*.label, the SAME source as the rail — never a literal, which is how "Code" and "Código" diverged. The PRs tab note does NOT promise to unlock on its own: the Código tab asks for the state and revalidates, the PR list only discovers the 409 when the query fails.

    /containers without a container. Parar and Remover were already disabled with registrado: null (RN-521, "never provisioned") and with removed, but nothing said why, and the danger variant of Button had no disabled state — "Remover" came out in solid red, the same as the active one. Now the row states the reason ONCE in text (Nunca provisionado: não há container para parar nem remover. / Container removido: …), and .danger:disabled gets the same opacity as the other variants. A row with a running container gets no text at all.

    The long reason for the missing broker. The text that names BROKER_URL was nine lines long and tripled the height of the table row. The cell now shows ONE short sentence in a <summary>, and the long text (which still names the variable, for the operator) opens on demand in the <details> — native and keyboard accessible, never a title on a disabled button (ADR 0064). When the SAME missing broker blocks start AND stop/remove, it is ONE line only ("subir, parar e remover só terminariam em falha"), with both details behind the same <details>.

    • Code: apps/web/src/components/ContainerImageGate.tsx:33 (ContainerImageGateNotice), :42 (bloco); apps/web/src/routes/ProjectPrsTab.tsx:157 (superficie="prs"); apps/web/src/routes/ContainersPage.tsx:261 (semContainer), :276 (chaveCurtaDoBroker); apps/web/src/components/ui/Button.module.css:101 (.danger:disabled)

    • Origin: AT-323, AT-324. Does not change the gate of RN-105/RN-494 (the PR list stays blocked without a decided image — whether it SHOULD escape the gate is the owner's decision), nor the start yardstick of RN-521/RN-574.

    RN-648 — The sidebar, the Dashboard card and the artifacts panel do not contradict each other: each empty state says WHICH session it read, and counter and list count in the same unit

    Three screens asserted "nothing" next to a screen that showed something (AT-325 — findings S2/D2/X6 of the visual survey of AT-290). Reproduction with the real engine was not possible in this environment (egress blocks repo.hex.pm and Alpine, and no image compiles); the three contradictions were MEASURED in the code and proven by tests that fail before the fix. The Dashboard one was born, in the survey, from a 500 of the engine stub in the workspace summary — but the text that turned it into "no activity" is the web's, and with the real engine any summary failure would produce the same.

    The sidebar read the EXECUTION session, and only it. The Atividades block (RN-198) requested the events of the current execution session (useActiveExecutionSession). In a project with only conversation sessions it is null, the list came back empty, and the sidebar said "Nenhum agente entrou em ação ainda" next to a Visão geral with the Criativo waiting. Now: with a current execution, the sidebar keeps reading IT (the dev agents and the -2 instances are what the grouping of RN-198 exists to show, and the queryKey is the Executores tab's); WITHOUT execution, it reads the SAME session as the Visão geral and the Dashboard card — the most recent working one (useLatestSession, the rule of sessaoMaisRecente). The states that collapsed into the same text separate (RN-470): loading (nothing is asserted), error (its own text), project without a session ("Este projeto ainda não tem sessão."), and the session read without an agent, with the text NAMING which one ("…na sessão de execução…" / "…na sessão mais recente…"). The session list is only requested without execution, and with the freshness of a project cycle (frescorMs in useProjectSessions): when the sidebar enables it the project frame has already brought it, and the request budget of RN-632/RN-645 does not grow.

    The Dashboard card had one text for four states. "Sem atividade ainda" came out with the workspace summary LOADING, with it FAILED (the survey's case: the summary responded 500 and the card of a project with sixteen events said there were none), with the project WITHOUT a session and with the most recent session empty. Now there are four texts: "carregando atividade…", "atividade indisponível", "Nenhuma sessão ainda" and "Sem atividade na sessão mais recente ainda". The row still reads the most recent working session (latestSessionId), the same as the Visão geral and the sidebar without execution — the api did not change.

    "Artefatos gerados 3" over a single group "PO 1". The section header counted the backlog TREE (epic, story and task: 3) and the per-agent group counted only the ROOTS (1). Both now count in the same unit — each item carries quantos (1, plus the descendants when it is a backlog root), the header is the sum of the items and each group is the sum of its own, so the sum of the groups IS the header, by construction. And the section's empty state says what it counts ("Nenhuma PR nem item de backlog nesta sessão ainda.") instead of the generic "Nada ainda.", which next to a brief or a rule of the same session seemed to deny they existed — the section's scope (PR and backlog, RN-159) does not change.

    • Code: apps/web/src/routes/Shell.tsx:579 (execucao), :587 (origemDaAtividade), :594 (estadoDaAtividade); apps/web/src/lib/hooks.ts:127 (useProjectSessions, frescorMs); apps/web/src/routes/Dashboard.tsx:75 (lastActivityText); apps/web/src/routes/ContextAside.tsx:46 (quantos), :340 (totalDeArtefatos)

    Following live without hammering (RN-639)

    RN-651 — On the phone, the Session panel is a drawer, the bar wraps, and the table becomes cards

    The "after" visual audit of Round 29 (docs/explanation/auditoria-visual-rodada-29.md, findings N1 and N3 to N8) measured, at 390px, what RN-643 had not reached: in the Session the "Contexto da sessão" panel kept ~320px and left ~70px to the thread (one word per line), and the bar pushed "Iniciar ideação" and "Encerrar" past the edge; in /containers seven fractional columns overlapped the headers and cut the actions; in "Modelos por agente" the agent name had 6px and the selector 0px; /status scrolled sideways (413px); the Dashboard search became "Bus"; the session name in the Criativo's list had 0px; and the mobile rail left the active tab cut off, with no sign that it scrolled.

    The rule (the cut is the same useLayoutMovel of RN-643, and the design comes through a CLASS or attribute that the component sets, never through @media):

    1. The Session context panel is born CLOSED on the phone and opens as a DRAWER over the thread, from the right: a modal dialog with the panel's name, focus on the X on opening, Esc, X and backdrop close it and return focus to whoever opened it. On desktop it is born open next to the thread, as always. Crossing the cut goes back to the new side's default — a panel open on desktop does not reappear squeezing the thread on the phone. The exception is navigation that COMES to the panel (highlightEvent, the Psicólogo's evidence chip): then it is born open on the phone too.
    2. The Session bar wraps, never cuts. Exit, state, title and the panel toggle stay on the first line; type, settings, "Iniciar ideação" and "Encerrar" go down, and go down again if needed. What the bar shows is still decided by its width (RN-620 item 6).
    3. Every Table becomes a stack of cards on the phone. Each row is a card and each cell carries the column label next to the value; the header disappears. A column without a label, or marked largaNoMovel (a selector, a block of actions), takes the card's width with the label on top.
    4. The page does not scroll sideways, and what identifies the row does not vanish. /status scrolls the table inside its own wrapper; the Dashboard search gets a full line; the session list row wraps with the name on top, whole.
    5. The mobile rail follows the active tab and says it scrolls. The active one is brought into the strip on mount, on switch, when the rail's CONTENT changes (the counters arrive later and widen the earlier tabs) and when the fonts finish loading — never on every render, otherwise whoever scrolled the strip by hand would be pulled back. The edge that hides tabs fades (data-rola-inicio/data-rola-fim).

    Measured (390×844, both themes, native api with the seed, validacao-visual.js injected): scrollWidth 390 on all seven screens; zero texto-cortado on the Dashboard, the Criativo's list, the Session (with and without the drawer), /containers and /status; in Configurações the 33 dropped to zero, and the rail's active tab stayed whole within the strip on the three tabs measured.

    What this rule does NOT close: in the minimal bar "Encerrar" still has only the icon and the accessible name (RN-620 decides that by the bar's width). The panel toggle moves up to the title line only in PAINTING (order): the Tab order follows the DOM's. The fora-da-viewport that the checker still reports in Configurações and Aprovações are decorative icons with position: absolute inside controls below the fold, not something cut off.

    • Code: apps/web/src/lib/painel-de-contexto.ts:16 (usePainelDeContexto); apps/web/src/routes/GavetaDoContexto.tsx:14 (GavetaDoContexto); apps/web/src/routes/SessionTopbar.tsx:126 (movel); apps/web/src/components/ui/Table.tsx:13 (largaNoMovel), :28 (movel); apps/web/src/routes/Dashboard.tsx:165 (movel); apps/web/src/routes/ProjectSessionsTab.tsx:149 (movel)

The project tab strip (ProjectRail, finding N8) left in ADR 0211: the tabs live in the drawer

    The author of each utterance in the thread is the event's actor (RN-652)

    RN-652 — chat.message is drawn under the event's ACTOR — person, agent or unknown —, never under whoever is viewing the screen

    Until now every chat.message came out with the name and avatar of whoever was logged in: in a shared session, another person's utterance appeared with YOUR name, and an agent's with your e-mail (finding N2 of the visual audit of Round 29, hidden while the thread grouped the history by origin). The author now comes from the event's actor, which the api records with the sender's id (SendAgentMessageUseCase, SendChatMessageUseCase) and which the generic append-event route accepts of any kind. The outcomes do not collapse:

    1. The viewer (actor.id equal to the token's sub; without it, by the e-mail of the member row) — the name or e-mail of the member row, otherwise the session's e-mail, and only in the absence of both "Você".
    2. Another person the screen can name — their name, otherwise the e-mail, from TWO reads composed into a single list (comporMembros), the PROJECT one before the WORKSPACE one: GET projects/:id/members (minimum viewer), under the SAME queryKey as the Configurações tab, and since AT-335 GET workspaces/:id/members (RN-655), of the PROJECT's workspace. Whoever has both rows appears once, through the project one — the same order as the role overlay (RN-471).
    3. Another person the screen CANNOT name — "Outro membro": without a project or workspace row (someone who left the workspace and still has utterances in the log), or both reads failed or have not arrived yet. The read that fails does not erase the other, and neither of them breaks the thread. Until AT-335 this was the declared GAP of whoever joins only through the workspace role, which had no read route; the route was born by owner decision.
    4. Agent — nomeDoAgente and the agent's avatar/color, like agent.response.
    5. Unknown actor (absent, without id, system or a kind outside person/agent) — "Autor desconhecido", never "Você".

    The backlog.story_promotion_returned narration, which had the same defect (the viewer's name over whoever returned the story), goes through the same function. The composer's OPTIMISTIC message keeps the viewer's e-mail — it is always theirs.

    • Code: apps/web/src/lib/autor-da-mensagem.ts:52 (autorDaMensagem); apps/web/src/lib/autoria-da-sessao.ts:45 (useAutoriaDaSessao), :18 (comporMembros); apps/web/src/routes/session-timeline-montagem.tsx:154 (rotuloDoAutor), :405 (the chat.message), :687 (the story return); apps/web/src/routes/SessionPage.tsx:133 (autoria)
    • Test: apps/web/src/routes/SessionPage.autor-da-mensagem.test.tsx:147 (two people and one agent, each message under its own author — happy path), :168 (unknown actor and person outside the members with its own text — failure case), :186 (whoever joins only through the workspace role is named — AT-335), :205 (the refused workspace read does not erase the project's), :219 (refused member read); apps/web/src/lib/autoria-da-sessao.test.ts:15 (the project row before the workspace one), :22 (failure of one read does not erase the other); apps/web/src/lib/autor-da-mensagem.test.ts:58 (absent actor, without id or of an unknown kind never becomes "você")
    • Origin: AT-329 (finding N2 of docs/explanation/auditoria-visual-rodada-29.md); the workspace list, AT-335

    The policy reason on each Aprovações card (RN-656)

    RN-661 — Auto mode is OFFERED in batch to the team at the start of execution, and the offer says what it does not unlock

    In the Executores tab, with execution active and the execution team on screen, a card offers to switch on auto mode (RN-153) for the team's agents at once (AT-315). It is ONE control, and it is an offer: the agents not yet in auto come checked, the person unchecks whoever they do not want, and nothing is recorded without the click on "Ligar". The click records the SAME wildcard (agent_autonomy, actionType: "*", auto_approve) through the SAME endpoint as the per-agent toggle (PUT .../agent-autonomy, maintainer) — one PUT per agent, in series, without aborting on the first refusal, with a PER-agent outcome in the three cases of RN-469 (all, none with the api's sentence, some with who stayed in manual). Switching off remains in the manual/auto toggle of each agent's card; there is no "switch off in batch". When all are already in auto, the card disappears.

    The card STATES, before the click, what auto mode does NOT unlock — the absolute ceilings still ask for approval (RN-154, RN-418): merge into a protected branch; git push, opening a PR and deploy; sudo/doas; container_remove; instruction_patch; parallelizing or raising the parallelism ceiling. Without maintainer the control stays inert and the reason is stated in text; the role read is the WORKSPACE one, with the same declared gap as the other auto mode screens (RN-471).

    The runner flow through the browser is retired (RN-687, ADR 0203)

    RN-687 — The runner panel sends to the installer; the browser does not generate a key, does not download a binary and does not write a folder

    The flow of ADR 0118 (RN-464..466) was RETIRED by owner decision (10/01, BRB-031): it was the only installation path that ended in a manual chmod +x, and install.sh already does its three things on the right side — it downloads the binary checked against the signed checksums.txt and installs it with install -m 0755 (RN-531), and creates the key ON THE MACHINE and registers it (RN-547, RN-551, RN-552).

    The rule:

    1. The RunnerOnboardingPanel shows the installer command (curl -fsSLO …/install.sh && bash install.sh, the SAME sentence as the runbook and install.sh, RN-526), copyable, with what it does stated in text, the caveat that it serves the installation's machine, and the EsperaDoRunner (RN-474) below. The command in the web is a COPY, and the copy is checked against the installer's constant.
    2. Once an active key that serves the project is recognized — machine (RN-548) or project (AT-107) —, the installer block LEAVES and the gesture is checking the service. The manual command with a PAT (--token) always stays in the <details>, named as the path for ANOTHER machine.
    3. There is no button to configure a folder, to download files, nor a platform selector, and the web no longer calls Web Crypto, File System Access nor the binary proxy. runner-bootstrap.ts was removed entirely.
    4. The api no longer has POST /projects/:projectId/runner-device-keys. It was left without a caller. GET and DELETE stay (list and revoke); the internal machine route (POST /internal/machine-device-keys) stays.
    5. An ALREADY registered project key remains valid and revocable: nothing in the database changes, PatAuthGuard keeps accepting it (RN-465) and the CLI keeps reading the configured folder (RN-466). A runner configured through the browser before ADR 0203 does not break.

    Left out, declared: an installation with a TEAM loses the PAT-less path to pair a second machine (the machine key route is 409 with two or more people), and now uses the manual command; GET /runner-releases/binary is left without a production consumer and was NOT removed — that is a separate decision.

    • Where: apps/web/src/components/RunnerOnboardingPanel.tsx:68 (COMANDO_DO_INSTALADOR), :124 (RunnerOnboardingPanel); apps/api/src/interfaces/http/runner/runner-device-keys.controller.ts:75 (RunnerDeviceKeysController), :96 (listDeviceKeys), :119 (revokeDeviceKey); apps/api/src/interfaces/http/auth/pat-auth.guard.ts:152 (autenticarChaveDeDispositivo)
    • Test: apps/web/src/components/RunnerOnboardingPanel.test.tsx:91 (happy path: the installer command, copied, and the wait), :119 (the browser flow is gone: none of its buttons, no chmod), :134 (failure case: a refused clipboard does not break the screen), :211 (active machine key: the installer leaves); apps/api/test/interfaces/http/runner/runner-device-keys.controller.spec.ts:43 (there is no POST handler); apps/api/test/interfaces/pat-auth.guard.spec.ts:345 (project key still accepted); scripts/dev/install-invocacao.spec.ts:162 (the panel's command is the installer's sentence); scripts/ci/alvos-do-runner.spec.ts:70 (the browser's target list does not come back)
    • Architectural decision: ADR 0203

    The RN-418 ceiling also applies through the typed door (RN-689)

    RN-693 — Activating the agent is not conversation: the opening hint stays in the thread until the first message, and the session's text fields do not invite autofill

    Measured in the real use of v7.0.0: "Iniciar ideação" records agent.activated (and agent.status), the Criativo only speaks after the first message, and the invitation — with the hint "Comece contando o que você quer construir…" — vanished on the click, because the question "has the conversation started?" was events.length > 0. The thread stayed empty for ~25 s.

    The rule: agent LIFECYCLE events (agent.activated, agent.status) do not count as conversation. Any other event still counts, so the invitation does not cover the git-bootstrap session nor the execution one (RN-131). With the Criativo active and no message, the invitation stays without the "Iniciar ideação" button and with the hint; the first message removes it.

    And the composer and the text fields of the structured questions carry autocomplete="off" and the attributes password managers respect (data-1p-ignore, data-lpignore="true", data-bwignore, data-form-type="other"): each focus opened the browser's autofill popup.

    • Origin: AT-353

    RN-694 — The model catalog has search, the credential offers the sync, and the first model goes to the team in one click

    Three shortcuts over actions that already existed, no new endpoint (AT-357):

    1. Search. The catalog has a search field that matches the display name, the name or the id of the model, case-insensitively. With a term, every hub group and subgroup with a result opens by itself; a search with no result STATES that nothing matched, with the term — never a mute empty list.
    2. Sync next to the credential. The credential card whose provider has a key and NO model in the catalog says so and offers "Atualizar catálogo" — the SAME action as the catalog (POST .../models/sync). The button only appears for owner (the endpoint's minimum, RN-102); reading the catalog requires maintainer. The predicate is ONE, providersSemCatalogo, also used by the catalog's notice.
    3. Activate and apply to the team. When NO model is active and ONE is checked, the batch bar offers "Ativar e aplicar aos N agentes": it activates (POST .../models/activate) and, if activation passed, applies it to each agent through the same path as RN-476, with its overwrite notice and the partial report of RN-469. A refused activation applies nothing. Only with the click, never on its own, and only for owner (the higher of the two minimums); below that the control stays inert and the reason is stated in text. Curation stays manual (ADR 0042).

    The installer offers the local models, and does not mistake dev for an installation (RN-691, RN-692)

    RN-701 — The context panel does not assert "0" about rules that left the window

    The "Contexto da sessão" panel reads the events already downloaded (RN-180). In a session with more events than the window, the business rules emitted at the start fall BEFORE it, and the counter said 0 with "Nada ainda." — asserting about what it did not read (measured in the 268-event session of 10/02: 5 rules became 0). With events prior to the window, the counter now says "N na janela · há mais antes" and the empty section says there are none IN THE LOADED EVENTS. No new read: the number of earlier ones still comes from subtraction over the seq.

    The other half of AT-365 — the already executed approval card asking for a decision again — was NOT fixed here: the thread card already decides by the ACTION's status (ApprovalCard, isPending), and the actions read is the tail plus the pending ones (RN-637); the defect does not reproduce through the web code.

    Measurement (10/02, in the AT-015 environment): GET .../actions?latest=true of session 17d41c20 returns both open_adr_pr as executed, and reloading the session (with the collapsed blocks opened) shows no "Aprovar". The pending card only appeared DURING the live session — a transient state of the screen, not of the api nor of the window. The next AT-015 round captures the network at the moment it appears, before any fix.

    • Origin: AT-365

    RN-708 — A new project states the next step, derived from what is already read

    The Visão geral of a freshly created project did not say what to do: with no provider key, no model and no session, nothing on screen pointed the way, and the credential and the catalog lived at the end of Configurações (measured in the real test of 10/02, fresh installation). Since AT-372 the Visão geral opens with the "Primeiros passos" card, with THREE steps, each with the link to the exact place:

    • credential — the account has at least one LLM credential (the same ['credentials'] read as the Credenciais section, with configuration freshness and no poll, RN-645). The Visão geral did not make that read, and its load has a budget: it only runs when the team still has no model; with a resolved model, the step counts as covered;
    • model — the Criativo's resolved binding is not null (the batch of RN-654, the same queryKey as Configurações);
    • ideation — the project has at least one session (the list the page already polls).

    No new route. An input not read yet (or with an error) is "I don't know" (RN-470), and the card does NOT appear while there is one — asserting "credential missing" without having read it would be the screen lying. With all three done, it disappears. Along with it, the Configurações tab now opens on the models group, with the credential in front, and the summary follows the order.

    • Test: apps/web/src/lib/primeiros-passos.test.ts (happy path and "insumo não lido não vira pendente") and apps/web/src/routes/ProjectOverviewTab.test.tsx ("primeiros passos (RN-708, AT-372)")
    • Origin: AT-372

    RN-709 — The card uses the right verb tense, the ceiling does not offer "sempre permitir", and the plan states how much it costs and only starts agents with tasks

    Measured in the test project of 10/02. Four defects of the same decision card, closed together:

    • The verb follows the action's state (AT-371). The pending PR card said "abriu pull request (de ADR)" before opening. VERBO_DA_ACAO speaks of the PROPOSAL ("quer abrir pull request…") and VERBO_DA_ACAO_EXECUTADA gives the verb of what already happened, only for the types whose tense changes; verboDaAcao receives the status and is the single source.

    • ADR and infra PRs are an RN-418 ceiling (AT-371). open_adr_pr and open_infra_pr enter TIPOS_SEM_SEMPRE_PERMITIR (api and the web copy, checked by test), like pr_open — "sempre permitir" refuses to record the pattern and the card offers neither the button nor "Modo automático". Outside the terminal, the notes no longer speak of a command ("npm test", "roda qualquer comando"): the typed type has its own note.

    • The plan shows the estimate (AT-381). On proposing propose_execution_plan the api adds to the payload orcamentoPorTarefaMicros — the CURRENT per-task budget, the same one activation uses (project setting or the default of US$ 0.50) —, and the card's sentence says "até US$ X (N tarefas × US$ Y) — é o teto por tarefa, não o preço". No new route.

    • A module without tasks gets no agent (AT-381). Applying the plan passes to the activation the modules with ≥ 1 task (modulosDoPlano); those of the module_map without tasks stay out and are stated in the result (modulosSemTarefa), next to tarefasPorModulo. The "Ativar execução" button (without a plan) and a plan without tarefas still start the whole map. The computed summary reaches the Dev Lead in the tool result text (resumo_do_plano/1), so he can cite the numbers instead of recounting.

    • Where: apps/web/src/lib/aprovacoes.ts:428 (verboDaAcao), apps/api/src/domain/actions/sempre-permitir.ts:33 (TIPOS_SEM_SEMPRE_PERMITIR), apps/api/src/application/use-cases/actions/propose-action.use-case.ts:84 (execute), apps/api/src/application/use-cases/execution/execute-execution-plan.use-case.ts:89 (execute), apps/api/src/application/use-cases/execution/activate-execution.use-case.ts:123 (execute), apps/engine/lib/engine/agents/dev_lead_tools.ex:587 (resumo_do_plano)

    • Test: apps/web/src/lib/aprovacoes.test.ts ("AT-371 — o verbo segue o estado da ação", "AT-381 — o plano mostra a estimativa de gasto"), apps/web/src/components/ApprovalCard.test.tsx ("open_adr_pr pendente"), apps/api/test/domain/actions/sempre-permitir.spec.ts, apps/api/test/application/use-cases/execution/activate-execution.use-case.spec.ts ("módulos do plano"), apps/api/test/application/use-cases/execution/execute-execution-plan.use-case.spec.ts, apps/engine/test/engine/agents/dev_lead_tools_test.exs (resumo_do_plano/1)

    RN-712 — The session thread shows what just happened, once and legibly

    Measured in the real test of 10/02 (loja-teste): the turn strip showed raw Markdown, the structured form's answer appeared twice, the manual handoff selector offered Dev Lead, QA and Infra from the first minute of ideation, the "Para" stayed on the PO with the Arquiteto already working, the "Iniciar ideação" button reappeared mid-session and an 80-line docker-compose pushed the conversation off screen. Since AT-375:

    • turn strip — the preview and the expanded lines go through textoPlanoDoMarkdown: **, ##, rules, tables and backticks become running text. The strip is a preview; the rendered answer is still the bubble at the end of the turn (RN-460), and the streaming store does not change (RN-639);
    • answered form — the api resends the answers to the agent as the user's chat.message; the bubble whose text is EXACTLY what the api assembles ("<n>. <rótulo>: <resposta>"), the first user message after the answer, leaves the thread, because the answered card already shows them. A typed message that merely looks like a list stays;
    • recipient — the choice made in the composer's SELECTOR is manual and is never overwritten. One that came from a gesture (accepting a handoff on this screen, "Estou pronto") follows the target of the last ACCEPTED handoff, including one accepted by the system (RN-660). It revises the RN-631 clause that an acceptance through another path "only adds the option" — only for a choice that was not manual. With no choice at all and two options, sending stays locked;
    • manual handoff — the selector is born collapsed behind a button; the list of agents did not change;
    • "Iniciar ideação" — disappears from the header as soon as some agent has joined the session (agent event or handoff);
    • header — the time becomes "iniciada às HH:MM:SS", and the language origin wraps into up to two lines before ellipsizing, with the full text in title;
    • code block — above 20 lines it is born collapsed to the first 8, with "Mostrar as N linhas".

    Left out, measured: the Criativo's first "modelo não registrado" bubble comes from the ENGINE — criativo_server.ex records the accumulated text with a null modelName when the turn ends by delivering a form (gravar_texto_do_turno(state, "", nil)), not from the screen.

    • Where: apps/web/src/lib/texto-plano.ts:7 (textoPlanoDoMarkdown), apps/web/src/lib/eco-de-formulario.ts:17 (ecosDeRespostaEstruturada), apps/web/src/lib/session-destinatario.ts:237 (resolverDestinatario), apps/web/src/components/ui/MarkdownMessage.tsx:74 (CodeFence)

    • Origin: AT-375

    One cache key, one question (RN-718)

    RN-718 — Whoever writes a session's actions into the cache makes the SAME read as the thread

    ['session-actions', projectId, sessionId] is ONE TanStack Query cache entry, and the Sessões tab wrote into it the result of another question: the FIRST ascending page (listActions({ limit: 200 }), without latest), while the Session thread reads the tail of the 200 newest plus the pending ones it pushed out (RN-637). The two screens swapped each other's cut — after opening the tab, the thread could draw the session's 200 OLDEST actions, the defect RN-637 closed, reopened through the cache door (AT-365).

    The rule: key and function go together, in consultaDasAcoesDaSessao (apps/web/src/lib/acoes-da-sessao.ts). Whoever READS a session's actions to put them in the cache uses that pair — usePendingActions and the per-session summary of the Sessões tab. A key of its own for the tab was considered and discarded: it would stop the cache swap, but keep the tab summarizing the wrong cut. With the same read, the summary gains — a long session's new pending one now counts in "aguardando". Whoever only INVALIDATES the key (decision on the approval screens, the live channel) still writes the list by hand: invalidating writes no data.

    • Where: apps/web/src/lib/acoes-da-sessao.ts:76 (chaveDasAcoesDaSessao), :80 (consultaDasAcoesDaSessao); apps/web/src/lib/hooks.ts:432 (usePendingActions); apps/web/src/routes/ProjectSessionsTab.tsx:270 (acoesPorSessao)
    • Test: apps/web/src/routes/ProjectSessionsTab.test.tsx (describe "a leitura de ações é a mesma do fio": with the thread having written the tail, mounting the tab leaves tail + pending in the cache and no call without latest — fails on the old code, which wrote the 200 oldest; and the pending one pushed out of the tail counts in "aguardando")
    • Declared gap: the card of an action whose proposed_action.created left the 200-event window still JUMPS to the top of the thread (ordemDaAcaoNaTimeline degrades to createdAt and anchors at 0.5) — locked as current behavior in apps/web/src/routes/SessionPage.acao-fora-da-janela.test.tsx, whose fix is a separate decision. The same test proves that the decided card does not ask for a decision again: what seemed to "come back" in AT-365 was ANOTHER action.
    • Origin: AT-365

    The Anamnese does not pay twice for the same window, and gate rescue does not restart a cycle that waits for someone (RN-722)

    RN-728 — The user's message has a single bubble, and the Dev Lead card has a single primary

    • Rule: (1) the OPTIMISTIC bubble of the message sent to an agent leaves the thread as soon as the chat.message with the mensagemId the api returned on acceptance is in the log — matching by id, never by text, and without waiting for the end of the turn; its name comes from the SAME autorDaMensagem as the log bubble (RN-652), with the token's e-mail only as a fallback. Before, the two coexisted during the turn, one with the name and the other with the e-mail. (2) On the card "passou o bastão ao Dev Lead", accepting the handoff is the primary gesture and "Ativar execução" is SECONDARY, with the notice of what it skips: without a plan, the devs pick up the tasks as they are (RN-677).

    RN-736 — The "Nome do projeto" field does not get focus on its own nor is it called "name"

    • Rule: in the details step of the project wizard, the "Nome do projeto" field does NOT receive autoFocus and uses id/name project-title (since RN-834, projeto-slug), never plain "name"; the attributes that switch off password managers (data-1p-ignore, data-lpignore, data-bwignore, data-form-type="other") still come from the single source SEM_AUTOFILL (RN-729). Decision: no automatic focus in any case (not even by keyboard) — those extensions' "Contact Info" menu opens on FOCUS, ignores autocomplete="off" and covered the page; one more click or Tab is the price. The attribute names are those publicly documented by 1Password, LastPass, Bitwarden and Dashlane, not measured against each extension here.
    • Where: apps/web/src/routes/NewProjectWizard.tsx:746 (projeto-slug), apps/web/src/lib/conversa-comecou.ts:29 (SEM_AUTOFILL)

    • Origin: AT-418 (TP-01, 2nd round of 10/03)

    RN-740 — The "Nome do projeto" field declares that it suggests nothing, and the rest of the gap is the extension's

    • Rule: SEM_AUTOFILL adds aria-autocomplete="none" (the accessibility tree says the field offers no suggestion) and data-protonpass-ignore (the attribute Proton Pass documents). The visible label and the accessible name stay "Nome do projeto": changing only the aria-label would break "label in name" (WCAG 2.5.3), and changing the visible label is a product decision, not taken. Measured in TP-01 of 10/03: the "Contact Info" menu opens on focus/typing even with the RN-736 attributes — the extension probably classifies by the label TEXT. If the next TP-01 reproduces it with these attributes, the gap is the EXTENSION's and stays declared here.

    RN-747 — The Dev Lead's plan says how many dev agents start, not how many it asks for

    • Rule: approving propose_execution_plan activates execution (RN-677), and the activation starts ONE dev agent per module; the plan's per-module agentes is a recommendation. The approval card says "sobe N dev agent(s), um por módulo" and, when the plan asks for more than that, that the extra agents only start if the user accepts parallelizing later — parallelizing is an RN-154 ceiling and is never auto-approved nor bundled into the plan approval. The tool result that goes back to the Dev Lead says the same, and the description of agentes asks not to promise a parallelism gain. The model's quote marks at the ends of the summary are removed before the sentence cites it, so no coberto." is left over.

    RN-750 — The per-agent collapse leaves each agent's closing open

    • Rule: the per-agent message collapse after it passes the baton (RN-138) never folds that agent's LAST message in the thread — the closing, which in the handoff is the summary the person just read. What came before it in the same sequence is folded, and only with two or more entries (a single one does not become a header, RN-138's usual yardstick). Earlier sequences of the same agent, separated by another utterance, collapse as before. The thread's cut (RN-644) does not change: it runs over the already grouped list.

    RN-751 — The Session and the catalog do not jump: composer, active tab, batch bar and scrolling

    • Rule: four layout fixes, each a screen rule. (1) The composer does not squeeze the field during the turn: the buttons do not shrink nor wrap text, and move down a line when they do not fit. (2) Without the project frame mounted, the tab the sidebar marks is the one the ROUTE implies — on the Session screen, Chat —, and only outside that the default. (3) The model catalog's batch action bar OVERLAYS the list, pinned to the bottom of the window, and the section gets breathing room at the end while it is open; inserted in the flow, it pushed the list and the checkbox ran away from the cursor. (4) The thread "follows the end" (RN-173) also when the person WAS at the end before the height changed — the intent is measured on the scroll gesture, and a clamped scroll with content smaller than the window does not change it. The 120px guard remains: whoever scrolled up is not dragged. The cause of the top on accepting the Infra's handoff was not reproduced in the browser; the fix covers the class (content that shrinks and comes back).

Approving the card along with switching on the mode would be the owner's decision and was NOT done.

    RN-768 — In the PRs tab, the text next to Merge tells the truth about the button

    • Rule: a pending gate remains a NOTICE and does not lock the merge (RN-663). A BLOCKED task (task.blocked) is something else and disables the button; in that case the PR row does NOT show "o merge segue disponível": it says, in text, that the merge is unavailable because the task is blocked (naming the pending gate, if any, or the block's reason). Without a session in the project, the same: the reason goes in text, never only in the title of the disabled button, which does not open in Chromium. Whether a blocked task should or should not lock the merge is the owner's question, not decided here.

    RN-769 — Closing a session with active execution asks first, and the state appears translated

    • Rule: "Encerrar" in a session with active execution (an execution.activated event in the window, or a dev-<modulo> among the activated ones of the whole session — the Dev Lead does not count, he plans before execution) opens a confirmation that says what stops: the session's agents stop immediately, the running command is cut, the turn in progress is not recorded, and a session with execution does not reopen (RN-650). Without execution, the click closes directly, as before. The closed-session sentence in the composer interpolates the TRANSLATED state (pontoDaSessao(...).rotuloKey), never the raw enum (closed). And that is guarded in CODE, not only in the locale: the vocabulary yardstick reads the JSONs, and the enum came in through interpolation at runtime; i18n-enum-cru.test.ts rejects t(…, { status: x.status }).

    • Origin: AT-503 (chat UX note §3)

    RN-833 — Every web Phoenix socket gets disposed of, and the replaced one does not act

    • Rule: every web Phoenix socket is born through criarSocket and dies through descartar(), which does disconnect() and removes the three listeners that the phoenix@1.8.9 constructor registers on window (pagehide, pageshow, visibilitychange) — disconnect() alone left them, and the visibilitychange reopened the old socket. In the session channel each conectar() is a GENERATION: the previous one is disposed of (leave, descartar() and ITS ping) before the new one is born, and every callback of an old generation is a no-op — the onClose of a replaced socket does not schedule a reconnection, does not clear the current one's ping and does not mark the live channel dead. The live channel (RN-579) is a COUNT of connections per session, not a boolean. The neutralization of auto-reconnect (RN-108) stays mandatory in the type of criarSocket.
    • Where: apps/web/src/lib/socket-phoenix.ts:31 (criarSocket), apps/web/src/lib/session-channel.ts:92 (descartarGeracao), apps/web/src/lib/session-channel.ts:136 (ehAtual), apps/web/src/lib/canal-vivo.ts:70 (marcarCanalDaSessao)
    • Test: apps/web/src/lib/socket-phoenix.test.ts (real Phoenix: "50× criar + descartar deixa saldo líquido 0 nos três ouvintes de window"; failure: "socket só desconectado (sem descartar) deixa os três ouvintes"), apps/web/src/lib/session-channel.test.ts ("gerações (AT-500, RN-833)"), apps/web/src/lib/canal-vivo.test.ts
    • Origin: AT-500

The visible label "Nome do projeto" (linked by htmlFor) and the SEM_AUTOFILL of RN-740 stay.     RN-837 — Thread Markdown: new blocks and a stream prefix without raw markers

    • Rule: the chat's light Markdown (its own parser, no dependency) also recognizes horizontal rules (---, ***, ___; right after paragraph text it stays text), blockquotes (consecutive > lines, the inside reprocessed by the same parser), the #### heading, a nested list of ONE level (an item indented by 2+ spaces goes into the previous item; deeper is flattened) and checklists (- [ ]/- [x], a visual-only, disabled box). In stream mode (MarkdownMessage with emCurso), the text first goes through fecharMarcadoresAbertos, which closes pending fences, backticks and ** and removes a loose marker at the tip — no prefix throws nor shows a raw marker. The final text never goes through it. Still without dangerouslySetInnerHTML.

    RN-841 — The "PRs prontas" queue rereads itself without reloading the page

    • Rule: extension of RN-825. The list of open PRs still has no poll of its own, and is reread at two moments: (1) when the backlog read the chat already does at 15 s brings some task with a status or gateStatus different from the previous read (the dev's PR is born in the EXECUTION session, whose channel the chat does not listen to, and the gates approving show up there); and (2) when this session's channel announces adr.pr_opened, infra.pr_opened, action.pr_open, action.git_merge or action.failed, through the invalidator with a 2 s window (RN-579, RN-632). The first read and an equal read reread nothing: the cost is zero new polls. Left out, declared: an infra PR opened in another session without changing a task nor going through this channel only shows up on the next read triggered by another reason.

    RN-843 — One decision card per action in the chat

    • Rule: extension of RN-822. The read is a configuration one (FRESCOR_DA_CONFIGURACAO_MS, no poll) and the session channel's infra.pr_opened notice rereads it.
    • Where: apps/web/src/components/RodarNaSuaMaquina.tsx:32 (RodarNaSuaMaquina), :20 (urlLocalSegura), :13 (releSeInfraAbriuPr), apps/web/src/routes/ProjectOverviewTab.tsx:202 (releSeInfraAbriuPr), apps/web/src/lib/api-client.ts:832 (getRunInstructions)
    • Test: apps/web/src/components/RodarNaSuaMaquina.test.tsx ("mostra comando, copiar, link local e a nota de não verificado"; "sem declaração (null) o cartão não aparece"; "só localhost/127.0.0.1 com porta vira link"; "infra.pr_opened relê o cartão; outro evento não")
    • Origin: AT-517 (ADR 0213)

    RN-861 — Open questions are their own queue in the chat, and the readiness notice does not block

    • Rule: the chat's pending block gains the "Perguntas em aberto" queue, with its OWN title and count (the api's total, never summed with the other queues — RN-467) and a declared cut when the list comes with a ceiling (RN-180). Each card states the question, the agent that asked, what it affects, the suggestion with "Aceitar sugestão" and a short field with "Responder"; the task the QA gate parked because of the question (RN-860) appears on the card itself as "esperando sua resposta". Only the author of the originating session (autorDaSessao) has the controls; the others see the card with the control inert and the reason in text (ADR 0064). The refusal shows the api's sentence; success rereads the queue and the question leaves. The read does NOT poll: the channel rereads the queue on artifact.open_question, open_question.answered and pr.gate_changed (target perguntas, 2 s window). Next to "Estou pronto" and "Confirmar arquitetura pronta", the composer notifies "N perguntas em aberto" with a shortcut to the queue, reading the SAME query — it only notifies, the buttons stay enabled (ADR 0214).
    • Where: apps/web/src/components/PerguntasEmAberto.tsx:47 (PerguntasEmAberto), :117 (eAutor), :282 (AvisoDePerguntasEmAberto), apps/web/src/lib/canal-vivo.ts:135 (alvosDoEvento)
    • Test: apps/web/src/components/PerguntasEmAberto.test.tsx ("fila própria com contagem própria; o autor responde e aceita a sugestão", "o aviso do composer diz N e não bloqueia nada; some sem perguntas"; failure: "a recusa 409 mostra a frase da api", "quem não é o autor da sessão vê o cartão com o controle inerte e o motivo em texto")
    • Origin: AT-521

    RN-867 — The parked gate notice states the recorded reason, and the open question points to its card

    • Rule: gate.rescue_parked has two emitters: the rescue by time (GateRescuer, RN-722), which records idadeSegundos, and QA parked waiting for the answer to an open question, which does not record it. The thread tells the two apart by the PRESENCE of idadeSegundos (the motivo is fixed pt-BR text and is not read as a key): "parado mais de 2 h" only in the rescue by time; in the other, the notice says the gate is waiting for the answer and points to the "Perguntas em aberto" card (RN-861). The "Retomar gate" button (RN-724) stays in both.
    • Where: apps/web/src/routes/RetomarGateEstacionado.tsx:18 (cicloEstacionado), apps/web/src/routes/session-timeline-montagem.tsx (gateEstacionado.avisoPergunta)
    • Test: apps/web/src/routes/RetomarGateEstacionado.test.tsx ("o resgate por tempo grava idadeSegundos e lê como tempo"; failure: "a pergunta em aberto não tem idadeSegundos e não fala em 2 h")
    • Origin: AT-528

    RN-882 — The Session composer does not lose keystrokes during fast typing

    • Rule: the composer field owns ITS OWN value while typing: the drawn value is the local state, updated in onChange, and the page's draft receives each value (send, queue and buttons keep reading from it). A late draft that comes back as a prop NEVER rewrites the field — only a value the field did not emit is adopted (clearing after sending, the text returned on failure, the invitation's example). Before, the <textarea> was controlled directly by the SessionPage's draft, and the page's late re-render rewrote the field with an older value: 991 of 1,010 characters in TP-01, with an ordinary textarea on the same page receiving 249/249. Enter, Shift+Enter and accent composition (RN-830) do not change.
    • Where: apps/web/src/components/CampoDoComposer.tsx:21 (CampoDoComposer); apps/web/src/routes/SessionComposer.tsx
    • Test: apps/web/src/components/CampoDoComposer.test.tsx ("digitação rápida com a página atrasada não perde tecla nenhuma"; failure/edge case: "a escrita de fora (esvaziar depois do envio) é adotada pelo campo")
    • Origin: AT-533

    RN-883 — The auto mode batch offer also in the Session chat

    • Rule: with the session active and with execution (sessaoTemExecucao), the chat shows the SAME batch offer as the Executores tab (RN-661) — the same component, the same endpoint (one PUT per agent), the same text about what auto mode does not unlock, and nothing is recorded without the click. The agents are the dev-<modulo> that joined the whole session plus the gate subagents (agentesDaOfertaEmLote); role maintainer (the endpoint's). Without execution, nothing appears and agent_autonomy is not even read. It disappears when all are already in auto.
    • Where: apps/web/src/components/ModoAutomaticoNoChat.tsx:41 (ModoAutomaticoNoChat), :18 (agentesDoChat)
    • Test: apps/web/src/components/ModoAutomaticoNoChat.test.tsx ("caminho feliz: com execução, a oferta aparece e só o clique grava"; failure: "sem execução, nada aparece e nem a leitura acontece")
    • Origin: AT-537

    RN-884 — The answered question offers "Corrigir a história"

    • Rule: a question answered through the chat's "Perguntas em aberto" panel (RN-861) stays as an answered card until dismissed, and each story of afeta.historias that the backlog brings gets "Corrigir a história" when it is still correctable by RN-727's yardstick (draft, no in_progress/in_review task, role developer) — the link leads to the Histórias tab with it open (?historia=). It does not trigger the PO (owner decision, 10/14). Not correctable: the link does not exist and the reason is stated in text (RN-102). The yardstick is the SAME function as the Histórias tab's, moved to lib/.
    • Where: apps/web/src/components/PerguntasEmAberto.tsx:222 (CartaoRespondido); apps/web/src/lib/correcao-de-historia.ts (motivoDeCorrecaoInerte); apps/web/src/routes/ProjectBacklogTab.tsx:398 (useHistoriaInicial)
    • Test: apps/web/src/components/PerguntasRespondidas.test.tsx ("caminho feliz: história draft sem tarefa em execução ganha o link para a aba Histórias"; failure: "com tarefa em execução o botão não existe e o motivo é dito em texto")
    • Origin: AT-538

    RN-887 — The task block is orthogonal to the status, and whoever shows it reads both

    • Rule: a blocked task stays todo/in_progress with tasks.blocked = true (and blocked_reason/blocked_origin) — there is no blocked status in the enum, by owner decision (10/14). Whoever COUNTS or SHOWS a task reads both fields, and blocked wins over status: the Histórias tab shows the "Bloqueada" badge (danger tone) with the reason in text, and the others by status (to do, in progress, in review, done); the backlog task list that the Dev Lead reads marks the row with | BLOQUEADA: <motivo, até 120 caracteres>, and the task stays in the list. Claim, unblock and resumption do not change.
    • Where: apps/web/src/lib/execution.ts:10 (estadoDaTarefa); apps/web/src/routes/ProjectBacklogTab.tsx (the task list badge); apps/engine/lib/engine/agents/dev_lead_tools.ex:410 (bloqueio)
    • Test: apps/web/src/lib/execution.test.ts ("blocked vence o status"; failure: "sem bloqueio, o status"); apps/web/src/routes/ProjectBacklogTab.test.tsx ("a bloqueada mostra "Bloqueada" e o motivo, sem o selo "a fazer""); apps/engine/test/engine/agents/dev_lead_tools_test.exs ("a tarefa bloqueada diz BLOQUEADA e o motivo; a todo normal não")
    • Origin: AT-536

    RN-889 — The auto mode batch offer appears in the chat of any active session of a project in execution

    • Rule: extends RN-883. Approving the Dev Lead's plan creates the EXECUTION session, separate from the Criativo's, and it is in the Criativo's chat that the user drives. The batch offer (RN-661) appears in the chat of every ACTIVE session when the PROJECT has execution — roster.executionActivated of the projects summary (the most recent session, the execution one), the SAME read and queryKey the chat already made (useAtivosNoProjeto, RN-633), with no new query nor new poll. The dev agents from there (roster.activatedAgents) add to this session's. Same component, same endpoint, same text, nothing recorded without the click, it waits to know who is already switched on (RN-803) and disappears with the whole team in auto. Without execution in the project, nothing appears and agent_autonomy is not even read. Declared gap: the signal is the most recent session's roster.executionActivated, which says it HAD execution.activated, not that it is still open; with the execution session already closed, the offer still appears in the Criativo's chat — declared, not fixed, because telling them apart would require a new read (the request budget).
    • Where: apps/web/src/components/ModoAutomaticoNoChat.tsx:29 (ofertaDoChat); apps/web/src/lib/session-destinatario.ts:174 (useExecucaoNoProjeto)
    • Test: apps/web/src/components/ModoAutomaticoNoChat.test.tsx ("caminho feliz: execução ativa noutra sessão do projeto, time em manual — a oferta aparece no chat do Criativo"; failures: "time todo já em automático: nada aparece", "sem execução no projeto: nada aparece e nem a leitura acontece")
    • Origin: AT-540

    RN-901 — The auto mode batch offer in the chat includes the dev agents of the approved plan

    • Rule: extends RN-889. roster.activatedAgents of the project summary NEVER carries a dev agent: the dev does not record agent.activated, only dev.started. The chat's batch offer (RN-883/889) read only that list and did not list the dev-<module> of the active execution, which the user had to turn on from the Executors tab. The project execution (execucaoDoRoster) now adds, only with roster.executionActivated, the dev-<module> of roster.moduleNames (the current module_map), through the SAME devAgentId the Executors tab uses (RN-661, rosterFromFacts), with no new derivation. Without an active execution no dev enters. Declared gap: the list comes from the CURRENT module_map; if the approved plan used a subset of the modules, the offer inherits that divergence. No ceiling moves and nothing is written without the click.
    • Where: apps/web/src/lib/session-destinatario.ts:194 (execucaoDoRoster), called by useExecucaoNoProjeto (apps/web/src/lib/session-destinatario.ts:175)
    • Test: apps/web/src/lib/session-destinatario.test.ts ("CAMINHO FELIZ: execução ativa soma o dev- do module_map aos ativados"; failure: "sem execução ativa, o módulo do plano NÃO vira dev agent"), and apps/web/src/components/ModoAutomaticoNoChat.test.tsx (fixture derived from the real roster, "caminho feliz")
    • Origin: AT-573 (TP-01 18th, 19/10: the 1st task stopped at dev.awaiting_approval because the dev was not in auto mode)

    RN-911 — The provider is global; project Settings only choose a model; the matrix lives in the Account

    • Rule: LLM credentials, the model catalog (curation, prices, sync and the Hugging Face browser) and the spend report per key have left the project Settings. They live in /providers (the three pieces), and the spend per key also in the Spend tab, only for owner (RN-060). The project keeps only the CHOICE: model per agent and per area, the "apply to all" (RN-476), the routing criterion and "best models". The "Who can approve what" matrix has left Settings and is a section of the Account (/account), for anyone logged in. The "Enable and apply to the N agents" shortcut (RN-694 item 3) no longer exists. An old link ?section=credentials, model-catalog, key-spend or approval-matrix opens the tab at the top, without error. The empty state of "Models per agent", the subtitle of "best models" and the First steps (RN-708) point to /providers. Revises RN-694 (item 3) and RN-708 (destinations). No ADR: it is a screen boundary.
    • Where: apps/web/src/routes/settings/sumario.ts:48 (SECOES_DE_CONFIGURACOES), apps/web/src/routes/ProjectSettingsTab.tsx:62 (ProjectSettingsTab), apps/web/src/routes/AccountPage.tsx:36 (AccountPage)
    • Test: apps/web/src/routes/settings/sumario.test.ts (the four keys do not exist); apps/web/src/routes/settings/SumarioDeConfiguracoes.test.tsx (the tab does not mount the four sections, not even for owner); apps/web/src/routes/AccountPage.test.tsx (the matrix, and the failure case in the language); apps/web/src/routes/configuracoes.orcamento.test.tsx (returning to the tab does not fetch the credential); apps/web/src/components/catalogo-com-busca.test.tsx (no shortcut)
    • Origin: AT-597 and AT-598 (EP-031), owner's requests of 22/10.

    RN-912 — The area model is a box on the lead's line, under "Models per agent"

    • Rule: the "Area model" section no longer exists. The area default (RN-102, the area scope of RN-470) is linked by a box only on the lead's line of each area (dev-lead, qa, infra): "Apply to the whole X area (lead + subagents)". The area binding is one value per area and does not store the source agent, so a box on every line would have two competing for the value. Checked ⇔ the lead's effective value comes from the area (origin: 'area'), read from the batch of RN-654, with no new query. Checking writes the area with the lead's effective value and the lead's routing criterion (PUT .../area-bindings/:area) and THEN deletes the lead's own binding (DELETE .../agent-bindings/:lead; copying it would leave it behind, RN-102). These are two calls and the outcome is per step (RN-469): if the area refuses (including the 422 of RN-040) nothing changed and the toast carries the API's phrase; if the area was written and the DELETE fails, the screen says the area default was written and the lead's own model was not removed (a 404 on the DELETE counts as already removed). Unchecking deletes the area (DELETE .../area-bindings/:area) and writes nothing to the lead; a 404 becomes "already inherited". With the box checked, the lead's selector and routing criterion write the AREA. An agent with its own binding overrides the area (RN-470) and does NOT follow: the screen lists, in plural, the known subagents in that situation (declared scope, RN-180; the dynamic dev-<modulo> ones are not included and the screen says so) and does not delete their decision. The "apply to all" (RN-476) writes its own binding to the 17 and, for that reason, takes the subagents off the area default; its notice says so. Role: the box and, while checked, the lead's selector require maintainer (the minimum of the area endpoint, RN-102), compared by roleAtLeast; below that they stay inert and the reason is said ONCE in text in the section legend (ADR 0064). The other lines remain at developer. The box is born disabled if the lead has no effective value to copy, and it says the reason in text on the box itself (AT-601, key modelsSection.areaBox.needsModel). Revises the UI of RN-040, RN-102 and RN-470; the cascade, the scope and the endpoints do not change (no ADR).
    • Where: apps/web/src/routes/settings/ModelsSection.tsx:94 (podeEditarArea), :428 (areaDoLead), :446 (handleMarcarArea), :486 (handleDesmarcarArea)
    • Test: apps/web/src/routes/settings/ModelsSection.caixa-da-area.test.tsx (AT-601: reason when the lead has no effective value); apps/web/src/routes/ProjectSettingsTab.test.tsx (checking, the area refusing, PARTIAL FAILURE, unchecking with and without a 404, the lead's selector on the area, non-followers, developer inert); apps/web/src/routes/settings/papel-na-tabela-de-agentes.test.tsx (the two minimums); bindings-em-lote.test.tsx (single batch); cascata.test.tsx; heranca.test.tsx; filtro-de-aptos.test.tsx (RN-040); preferencia-de-roteamento.test.tsx (ADR 0166)
    • Origin: AT-600 (EP-031), owner's request of 22/10.
